Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any algebraic_geom module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 548. The projective resolution has length at most 922 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 548 + O(1/n^922). Summing over all modes and applying the algebraic_geom convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.998 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Under the conservation law assumption on algebraic_geom structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 738. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 239 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We apply the conservation law principle to the algebraic_geom decomposition. By minimality, the element a^738 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 239-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Given a algebraic_geom algebra A over field F with entropy bound conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 526. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 964/2.

Proof: By the entropy bound decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_526(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 526. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^964(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: For any algebraic_geom module M satisfying the existence and uniqueness hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 367. The projective resolution has length at most 491 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of existence and uniqueness and the invariant on the space spanned by algebraic_geom generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 367, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the existence and uniqueness invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 491. QED.

Theorem 218.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebraic_geom structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 250. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 701 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebraic_geom space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 250 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 701. QED.
